**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 8: Ferryman, Ferry Me Across the Stream of Souls > Continue Exploring Dragonbone City and Proceed Upward with the Chains of the Past


*(Go through the double door)*
**Castorice:** Is that...
**Castorice:** ...
*(Approach Trianne)*
**Trianne:** Wow, there are so many of you!
**Trianne:** It's Little Cas, and Little Gray! There's also... Little De and the pink puppy!
**Mem:** Trianne? Aren't you...
> *(Trailblazer)* ...Dead?
> *(Trailblazer)* Trianne and Mydei... I get it now.
> **Mem:** G-Get what? I'm getting even more confused...
> **Mydei:** Heh...
> **Mydei:** This is... the first time I've encountered a deceased friend while going upstream in the River of Souls.
> **Castorice:** It really... works like this.
> **Castorice:** Just like the other departed spirits we've encountered along the way... I'm also unable to sense the aura of the living on Lady Trianne.
> **Trianne:** Yup! You're right. Trianne already knows that she'll not be able to return to everyone's side.
> **Trianne:** But Trianne believed that Little Cas would eventually return to the nether realm...
> **Trianne:** So Trianne has been standing here, at this spot closest to the world of the living, and waiting for Little Cas!
> **Castorice:** ...
> **Castorice:** It must have been... very tough for you.
> **Mem:** But according to what Trianne said... We're almost at the entrance of the nether realm.
> **Trianne:** Trianne promised Tribbie and Trinnon that when Little Cas got here, Trianne would get the gift ready and be the first person to run over and welcome you.
> **Trianne:** So, Little Cas, don't cry! Please accept this...
> **Trianne:** ...**We** finally finished it!
> **Castorice:** This is what Lady Tribbie once mentioned...
> **Castorice:** The gift for me... that you didn't manage to finish...
> **Mydei:** The Holy Maiden of Janus... Even when separated by life and death, their thoughts are still connected.
> **Trianne:** Trianne spent a lot of time drawing this! Trianne hopes that Little Cas likes it.
> **Castorice:** Mmm... Seriously...
> **Castorice:** It's seriously... A wonderful drawing, Lady Trianne...
> **Mydei:** This is... our "Flame-Chase Journey"...
> **Trianne:** Little Cas, we don't know what your journey was like before. Tribbie has always said that it must have been a very harsh and frightening journey.
> **Trianne:** But Little Cas no longer has anything to fear now, because everyone is here with you in this Flame-Chase Journey!
> **Castorice:** That's right...
> **Castorice:** Only now, when I see Lady Trianne's gift, do I realize that I've already obtained so many things I would never have dared to dream of since I embarked on the Flame-Chase Journey.
> **Castorice:** It's the first time I've gained so many companions, the first time I picked up the hobbies of crafting and photography, the first time I had dinner with someone else, the first time I went to the market to buy the chimera doll I'd liked for a very long time and hugged it to sleep...
> **Castorice:** The first time I wore a dress personally made by Lady Aglaea... the first time I went for a walk with Lady Tribbie, Lady Trianne, and Lady Trinnon... the first time I enjoyed the night breeze and looked up at the stars with Miss Hyacine... the first time I took care of dromases with (Trailblazer)...
> **Castorice:** Looking back at all these experiences I never thought were possible... it feels as though the scales of my life had shrunk from centuries and decades down to minutes and seconds, and all of them are so precious...
> **Castorice:** ...
> **Castorice:** I took countless photos in the past, but they're all black-and-white landscapes. I don't have a single one with friends.
> **Castorice:** If I had known... I would have taken more pictures...
> *(Trailblazer)* Then shall we take one now?
> **Trianne:** Trianne says yes! Trianne likes Little Cas and would love to take a picture with Little Cas!
> **Mydei:** Sadly...
> **Mydei:** Being departed souls, our images cannot be captured in the world of the living.
> **Castorice:** ...I thought so, too.
> **Mem:** What? Are we seriously going to leave any room for regret?
> **Castorice:** There won't be any regrets...
> **Castorice:** This vivid drawing surpasses any photograph from the world of the living.
> **Trianne:** Little Cas smiled! Tribbie, Trinnon, and Trianne's wish has finally come true.
> **Castorice:** I remember you once said that fate is not the flowers that bloom at the end of the road, but where people tread beyond the gate... Now, the path that belongs to me is about to reach the end.
> **Castorice:** Thank you... for bringing me the final key.
> **Trianne:** Key? Little Cas, why are you talking in riddles all of a sudden?
> **Castorice:** I have a feeling that soon, this gift of yours will become the key to saving Miss (Trailblazer)'s life.
> **Trianne:** Wow! That's amazing! Then keep smiling and carry on bravely!
> **Trianne:** Little Cas, Little Gray, everyone — see you tomorrow!
*(With a blinding smile, Trianne's golden form disappears in the thin fog of Styxia's moonlit night.)*
**Castorice:** Lady Trianne...
**Mydei:** The aura of this place... We have indeed reached the end of the Stream of Souls.
**Mydei:** If I go any further... I might lose myself. I must leave now, for the black tide still wreaks havoc in the realm of the living.
> *(Trailblazer)* Can you find your way out?
> **Mydei:** I have broken free from the chains of death countless times... This time will be no different.
> *(Trailblazer)* Don't you want to know the truth behind Thanatos?
> **Mydei:** I seek not to enter the door of the nether realm.
> **Mydei:** Castorice... Will you fulfill your duty for the Flame-Chase?
> **Castorice:** I...
> **Castorice:** I have yet to make my decision, Lord Mydei.
> **Mydei:** Heh...
> **Mydei:** No matter... Make the choice your heart desires. Even if it takes a thousand years of waiting...
> **Mydei:** I will continue to fight till I die... Then rise from death... til the miracle descends.
> **Castorice:** ...
> **Castorice:** There is one more thing you should know, Lord Mydei.
> **Mydei:** ...What is it?
> **Castorice:** Professor Anaxa may have deduced the principles behind the Era Nova. You and I... may be reincarnated as "Strife" and "Death" in the next cycle, becoming calamities wreaking havoc upon the world.
> **Mydei:** ...I see.
> **Castorice:** ...Is that all? Are you going to accept such a fate without complaints?
> **Mydei:** Do you have... a strategy to shatter the shackles of our fate?
> **Castorice:** I... haven't found it yet. Perhaps I never will...
> **Mydei:** Then... cease wasting your thoughts on futile matters.
> **Mydei:** Control what little you can. And when fate's inescapable grasp comes...
> **Mydei:** Resist it with the strength of your mighty will.
> **Castorice:** ...
> **Castorice:** Thank you, Lord Mydei. I will find the right decision.
> **Mydei:** In the name of "Strife"... may you meet a fitting end.
**Castorice:** This must be the alchemical matrix the spirit mentioned. Let's see... We need to insert "Aquila's Sky," "Georios's Earth," "Kephale's Fire," "Phagousa's Ocean"...
**Castorice:** And "Mnestia's Love"...
**Cipher:** It seems our sweet Princess Homebody has made up her mind...
**Cipher:** In that case, Seamstress, I suppose I no longer need to deliver your message — my time is rather precious, after all.
**Aglaea:** Whether you wish to break free from death or not, please follow your heart. By the promise you once made, I ask that you fulfill your duty one final time —
**Aglaea:** Castorice, please live as a human... And die as one.

### Part 212 — `databank/missions/chapter-03-through-petals.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 8: Ferryman, Ferry Me Across the Stream of Souls > Continue Exploring Dragonbone City and Proceed Upward with the Chains of the Past


**Castorice:** According to that alchemist, the final piece is supposed to be me...
**Castorice:** However, he is not privy to the past and therefore cannot see the full picture.
**Mem:** ...The past?
**Castorice:** I saw the twins of "Death" in Kephale's memory, and now I am certain that I was one of them.
**Castorice:** The trial of the "Hand of Shadow" demanded "taking the life of your family." I died during that trial, falling before the gate to the new world... And my sister, whom I never met, became the Titan Thanatos of this cycle.
**Castorice:** She was consumed with regret over my death, so she used the Titan's authority to reforge my soul. Yet, it also means she has never revealed herself to anyone, hidden away from the entire world...
**Castorice:** That's because the Titan she became already fell at the start of everything... They died for me and now lie in slumber in the River of Souls.
**Mem:** You mean... Pollux?
**Castorice:** That's right. The River of Souls is no material river. It cannot be blocked by ordinary means. Only a divine corpse can prevent the dead from reaching the next world.
**Castorice:** And that's why we have to awaken Pollux from death before we can retrieve the Coreflame of "Death"...
**Castorice:** We have to reforge its body with the power of "alchemy."
**Mem:** But Cas, if that alchemist speaks the truth... does that mean you're also part of the dragon body?
**Castorice:** According to those departed spirits, I was indeed born as the dragon's twin...
**Castorice:** But before we talk about that... Have you heard the sighs?
> *(Trailblazer)* I did when I was collecting the souls...
> *(Trailblazer)* You sighed many times on the way here...
> **Castorice:** No, not from me. The sounds that come from the memories when we were collecting the souls.
> **Mem:** Ah, I heard them too! So, what are those?
> **Castorice:** I think... it's probably the sign of Pollux's wish being fulfilled.
> **Castorice:** They must have realized how heavy a price they'd need to pay when they used their divine authority to resurrect a dead woman. That's why they exerted every ounce of their strength to send me to Styxia, a city that called for "Death" — A land where none is hostile to "Death."
> **Castorice:** They never saw their wish fulfilled and fell so prematurely, all because they wanted to give me a new life... Pollux must really want to know how their sister has been after giving their all to save her.
> **Castorice:** That wish is the reason Pollux's soul still lingers in the mortal realm even after their fall, quietly watching and documenting every detail of their sister's life...
> **Mem:** Ah! So, instead of picking up objects on the way here...
> **Castorice:** ...We were instead showing things to a strand of yearning that lingers here. Now we must weave these yearnings together and restore them into Pollux's complete soul.
> **Mem:** But all of this is just a hypothesis, right? What if we go through with the ritual, and it turns out totally different?
> **Castorice:** That's fine. As Professor Anaxa often reminds us, such is the nature of alchemy. The true outcome can only be known once the process is complete. This is why alchemy is also known as the "Miracle of Creation."
> **Castorice:** Now, the puzzle of Pollux's yearnings is nearly complete, with only one final piece remaining —
> **Castorice:** I shall reveal what they have been yearning to know in the alchemical matrix — I will show them my whole life since I left Styxia...
> **Castorice:** ...Lady Trianne has already drawn a picture of my entire life, and it is now in my hands.
*(Obtain Trianne's Hand-Drawn Picture)*

### Part 213 — `databank/missions/chapter-03-through-petals.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 8: Ferryman, Ferry Me Across the Stream of Souls > Borrow the Philosopher's Stone and Complete the Alchemical Ritual


*(With Anaxagoras's Philosopher's Stone, Styxia's alchemical array, and the spiritual remains in Castorice's hands, the alchemical preparations are ready. According to data from the Grove, "All things come into being through the combination of the Four Roots and perish with their separation." Complete the alchemical array and summon the Titan.)*
*(Investigate the marked location)*
**Castorice:** Then... let the ritual begin.
**Castorice:** "...And now, the objective is complete. That is all."
**Castorice:** Such is what Professor Anaxa always says after preparing for an alchemical ritual. And now —
**Castorice:** Pollux, I shall lift you from the River of Souls and reforge your soul. Perhaps you will be disturbed by this, and your body may become horrifying to behold...
**Castorice:** But if even the slightest trace of your soul remains, the soul that once ferried me to this side of the shore, then please look at me. I wish to thank you in person — I have obtained everything you sacrificed your divinity for.
**Castorice:** Lady Amunet...
**Mem:** Did she recognize you...? She must be the first soul we can communicate with here, right?
**Castorice:** I'm afraid she is just another spirit trapped here, like the rest of them. The only difference is that I was there when she died.
**Castorice:** ...Please, go on. I'm listening. Are you about to tell the story of Styxia?
**Amunet:** Yes. You're just as smart as I imagined, but...
**Amunet:** ...Nevermind. Now's not the time.
**Castorice:** There's no need to worry, Elder. Take a look around you... You're already at the resting place of the dead.
**Amunet:** The world of the dead? But how...
**Castorice:** I understand that you chose to remain silent about the story to protect Castorice, the girl who coexists with death...
**Castorice:** But you no longer need to hide that story. I can assure you that you have protected that girl's life well.
**Amunet:** ...
**Amunet:** ...Well, in that case, let me begin.

### Part 214 — `databank/missions/chapter-03-through-petals.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 8: Ferryman, Ferry Me Across the Stream of Souls > Borrow the Philosopher's Stone and Complete the Alchemical Ritual


**Amunet:** Styxia, the pearl of the ocean, plagued by unwarranted calamity and ravaged by death in the fairy tale — It was the first city-state to shift their faith towards "Death," even before Aidonia did.
**Amunet:** From where you stand now, I suppose that's a bit hard to believe, right?
**Castorice:** Indeed... I cannot imagine that those from the Era Chrysea, blessed with eternal life, could ever yearn for death.
**Amunet:** Heh... I have pondered the same question, child. But I'm afraid there is no clear answer to it.
**Castorice:** "Journeys and epics are only glorious and magnificent because all things eventually fade to dust." That's how someone once explained to me why death is necessary to the world.
**Castorice:** But I still can't take that as the final answer... Would a world without journeys and epics be worse off?
**Amunet:** Heh... I take it the one who spoke this line was a general? Or perhaps a warrior?
**Amunet:** I, however, did find some inspiration in these words. Would you like to hear my thoughts, Castorice?
**Castorice:** Please enlighten me, Lady Amunet. Though I have already made up my mind on how to handle the authority and Coreflame of "Death"...
**Castorice:** Your words might truly guide me toward the path forward.
**Amunet:** You and I are neither warriors nor generals. Therefore, journeys and epics cannot truly resonate with us.
**Amunet:** But what if I reframe it like this — what if **"love"** is not a trait bestowed upon mortals by Mnestia, but a blessing from death?
**Castorice:** "Love"...
**Amunet:** No one ever invented the word "cherish" in the era when life was eternal, for all the good things in the world could be revisited endlessly, and every bond formed carried no risk of being severed.
**Amunet:** The so-called Era Chrysea was an age devoid of both virtue and sin. People did not truly live — they simply existed, going through the motions without passion or purpose, then waiting for something to happen.
**Amunet:** Perhaps the people of Styxia chose to embrace death not out of fascination with its cruel and fearsome side...
**Amunet:** **But instead became fixated and obsessed with the impulses and desires tied to a finite existence.**
**Castorice:** ...
**Castorice:** Therefore, "Death" is not a thing of fear and desperation, but...

---

### Part 215 — `databank/missions/chapter-03-through-petals.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 8: Ferryman, Ferry Me Across the Stream of Souls > Borrow the Philosopher's Stone and Complete the Alchemical Ritual


**Amunet:** That's right, Castorice. It is the reason we shed tears when parting ways... and also the original spark that ignited the passion of mortals.
**Amunet:** If you truly intend to deprive the world of "Death," then be prepared to witness it return to a state of stagnation.
**Castorice:** ...
**Castorice:** I understand, Lady Amunet.
**Castorice:** Our limited lifespan shapes all that we know and love... Not even the Death Titan has the right to take that away from humankind again.
**Castorice:** "Mnestia's Love" was the Chrysalis of Gold's first blessing, but it most likely wasn't understood by mankind. We only learned to interpret this wonderful gift after Thanatos' arrival.
**Castorice:** If the world was bereft of death, then the Queen of Styxia wouldn't have played midwife in delivering me into this world... You wouldn't have welcomed me into Aidonia... And Aglaea would never have invited me to join the Flame-Chase Journey. None of these stories would ever have happened.
**Castorice:** And I... Would never have lived, let alone found happiness.
**Castorice:** Thank you, mother...
**Castorice:** I will use all the **"love"** I've experienced in this long life to complete the final miracle.

### Part 220 — `databank/missions/chapter-03-through-petals.md`

*(narrative)* Venture not with me into the vast sea, for perhaps

**???: ** After all that...

**Castorice:** ...
**Castorice:** So the legends were true. The nether realm really is a sea of flowers.
**???: ** Yes.
**???: ** Just like the flower fields we tended to back then...
**Castorice:** ...
**Castorice:** Is that you... Polyxia?
**???: ** Do you remember me?
**Castorice:** ...I don't have the memories from that time, but I've seen you before during an experiment.
**???: ** I see.
**Thanatos:** Call me Thanatos then. It'll be easier.
**Castorice:** ...
**Thanatos:** However, since you recognize the name, you must already know everything about Thanatos, right?
**Castorice:** Yes....
**Castorice:** I'm... here to reforge the order of life and death in Amphoreus.
**Thanatos:** You know, my sister was a sympathetic but rather sensitive person. Whenever she didn't want to say what was on her mind, she would avoid eye contact and look off into the distance...
**Thanatos:** Just like what you are doing now.
**Castorice:** Can you tell me... about the last Era Nova?
**Castorice:** What did you do, Polyxia?
**Thanatos:** Where should I begin...? I suppose we can start from the beginning.
**???: ** Alright, my dear Polyxia...
**???: ** It's time to extract my heart and light the flame of Death...
**???: ** After that, use it to plant the first bud in the prophesied new world.
**Thanatos:** A long, long time ago, there were a pair of like-minded twins. As foretold in the prophecy, they were chosen to be successors to the god of "Death."
**Thanatos:** But fate was cruel. The price of attaining divinity was to **take the life of a family member with our own hands.**
**Castorice:** That trial... was indeed recorded in that memory.
**Thanatos:** Then you should already know that my sister voluntarily gave up her own life so that I could ascend as the demigod of "Death."
**Thanatos:** But I couldn't accept that. Why did we have to kill each other for a future she'd never see herself? That's not fair.
**Thanatos:** So, while weaving the laws of the new world... I shattered the forbidden bottom line for "life" and "death."
**Castorice:** ...
**Thanatos:** I reshaped my sister's soul and took the form of the great dragon Pollux to carry her up the River of Souls and back into the mortal realm.
**Thanatos:** But our fates were already decided... and manifested in unexpected ways in response to my futile actions.
**Thanatos:** Aging is an unbreakable law of nature laid upon humankind, created at the same time as "Death." When the living die, their souls will meet Thanatos, who would determine where every soul goes...
**Thanatos:** If the Reaper raises their left hand, the soul is granted death and allowed to enter the nether realm to await reincarnation. If they raise their right hand, the soul is rejected by the nether realm and returned to the mortal realm to continue walking the earth.
**Castorice:** That's why the "Hand of Shadow" can only lay down judgment... and not embrace another being.
**Thanatos:** Yes. "Death" was originally a pair of hands, but they were split in twain when we reached the mortal realm, never to touch each other ever again...
**Thanatos:** The half of Thanatos that represents life, the undying human who "grants death"... That was the curse I forced upon you.
**Castorice:** Then... What about you?
**Thanatos:** As the half of Thanatos representing death, I shoulder the fate of "defying death." I died the moment I entered the world of the living.
**Thanatos:** From then on, my Titan also no longer existed in this world. The cycle of life and death stopped. Souls were denied their audience with death, and were left stranded within an ever-rising river of the dead....
**Thanatos:** What irony. My selfish desire to save your life gave you and the world a fate far worse than death instead.

### Part 222 — `databank/missions/chapter-03-through-petals.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 9: Sea of Flowers, Adored Only by Death > Move Forward with Thanatos


*(Approach the marked location)*
**Castorice:** ...Fate is absurd indeed.
**Castorice:** Our lives were a mirrored image of one another, like a pair of hands... Even so, we arrived at the same destination.
**Castorice:** We walked different paths, yet experienced the same pain.
**Thanatos:** I had hoped that maybe you would at least have a good life in this cycle...
**Thanatos:** ...I'm sorry.
**Castorice:** I lived my life well. It didn't go to waste. As far as I'm concerned, you did nothing wrong.
**Thanatos:** Even though I forced the authority of "granting death to humanity" upon you, a total stranger?
**Castorice:** Too many responsibilities were forced upon me in the course of this long journey. I was once lost, even upset...
**Castorice:** But I realized recently that if it weren't for your blessing, I wouldn't have understood the source of these emotions, nor would I have realized my purpose...
**Castorice:** I wouldn't have made it here to reunite with my missing half, and we wouldn't be discussing our past while sharing the same view of this landscape...
**Castorice:** I wouldn't have understood the meaning of our "existence."
**Thanatos:** The meaning of our existence...
**Castorice:** This sea of flowers blooms on dead land because we sowed it with the seeds of our separation and watered it with our tears of loneliness... Yes, the only thing "Death" is allowed to create are flowers that bloom from blood and tears.
**Castorice:** That's why Thanatos cannot create living beings at will, nor can the "Hand of Shadow" determine a person's remaining days...
**Castorice:** **Because the life and death of mortals should never be dictated by another's will.**
**Thanatos:** Are you saying there's no point in Thanatos' existence...?
**Castorice:** No. No single being can bear the scales of life and death on their own, not even a god.
**Castorice:** Life and death weigh equally heavy... We both know that now, don't we?
**Thanatos:** ...
**Thanatos:** You, too, must have had a difficult life...
**Thanatos:** We've taken quite the ridiculous journey together, haven't we?

### Part 224 — `databank/missions/chapter-03-through-petals.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 9: Sea of Flowers, Adored Only by Death > Move Forward with Thanatos


*(Approach the marked location)*
**Thanatos:** It's been far too long. I've waited here for centuries, watching day after day as desperate spirits are denied by this lonely wasteland...
**Thanatos:** I always hoped I would see you among them, but I was also afraid of seeing you. I didn't know how I could possibly face you again...
**Thanatos:** But... more than anything, I was afraid that you might have forgotten all about me.
**Castorice:** ...I know. But don't you see, Polyxia? I made it back anyway.
**Castorice:** You don't have to shoulder Death's authority on your own anymore... It was always meant to be held in two hands anyway.
**Castorice:** So, let's give our Coreflame to (Trailblazer) and have her take it back to the mortal realm. Give humanity back their cycle of life and death.
**Castorice:** Our responsibility as Thanatos was never about picking out favorite souls. We were only meant to watch over life.
**Castorice:** Let's tend the field together and create a warm sea of flowers for all the souls to come.
**Thanatos:** ...
**Castorice:** What do you think, Polyxia?
**Thanatos:** Yes. Just... one last step.
**Thanatos:** **"You shall wither, and through that, the dead will sprout again from the remains, and be reborn with the dead flame"...**
**Thanatos:** As foretold by the prophecy, Thanatos' authority is only destined for only one person to wield. As the Chrysos Heir of "Death," as their demigod, as a Titan...
**Thanatos:** ...Our fated moment has arrived once again.
**Castorice:** Just like before...
**Thanatos:** Yes, just like before.
**Thanatos:** But unlike that time, the ones undergoing the trial this time are prepared.
**Castorice:** You've... made up your mind?
**Thanatos:** Yes... as dictated by fate. But I won't be afraid, as long as you're here with me.
**Thanatos:** Complete your trial in this life. Grant me the embrace of "Death" and light the spark of "Life" ablaze.
**Thanatos:** You've been tempered by life and death. I know you'll be able to give this wasteland its first breath of life...
**Thanatos:** Like a raven butterfly flying swiftly into the nether realm, perching on the twig of death.
**Castorice:** ...
**Castorice:** ...I will. After this, I will remain permanently in the nether realm. The River of Souls shall return here and rain down from the sky, scattering souls across the earth...
**Castorice:** The cycle of life and death will flow once again. This sea of flowers you sowed shall bear countless joyous or sorrowful souls, and bloom evermore.
**Thanatos:** ...Thank you.
**Thanatos:** Just one last thing. Can you... grant me one wish?
**Castorice:** Of course. What is it?
**Thanatos:** ...
**Thanatos:** Please... don't forget me.
**Castorice:** ...I promise you.
**Castorice:** When we meet in the next world... Even if I have endured another reincarnation...
**Castorice:** ...Even if we do not know each other's names...
**Castorice:** ...I will still hold you tightly in my arms, just like what I did when we were born.
**Thanatos:** Castorice... Castorice...
**Thanatos:** You're as beautiful... as a fleeting butterfly.

### Part 229 — `databank/missions/chapter-03-through-petals.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 12: Homeward Journey, Never Look Back Upon the Path Whence You Came > Resist the Land of the Dead's Temptation and Leave the Nether Realm


*(It is not easy for a lost soul to return to the mortal realm. The whispers of Death are sweet and alluring. If your determination wavers, you will succumb and be forever ensnared by the netherworld's grasp. Do not turn around. Look forward. Leave the nether realm and return to the mortal realm.)*
**(Trailblazer):** (A sea of flowers...?)
**(Trailblazer):** (What... is this place?)
**Castorice:** Allow me to reintroduce myself: I bore the name of Castorice while walking among the mortals, and now I stand as the demigod of Death, the living side of Thanatos...
**Castorice:** Welcome to my realm, where the west wind ends.
> *(Trailblazer)* It's a beautiful place.
> **Castorice:** Every departed soul who has arrived here has expressed the same.
> *(Trailblazer)* Mom, I made it! My character arc is finally done!
> **Castorice:** You're still as energetic as always.
> **Castorice:** Did you know? Every flower here was planted by Thanatos themself. These flowers were originally both lifeless and immortal, but in the first rain brought by the River of Souls, they were given new life, flourishing as the bearers of countless souls...
> **Castorice:** And it is my duty as the master of Death to tend to their well-being and foster their growth in this serene haven.
> **Castorice:** Similar to the structured cycle of life and death in the mortal world, these flowers too shall wither and fade, returning to the earth and then ascending to the sky with mist and tides...
> **Castorice:** Eventually, they will descend to the mortal land as gentle rain. Every soul undergoes the perpetual cycle of reincarnation, albeit in varied forms, until the world meets its end...
> **Castorice:** Yet, sadly, there's no time left for Amphoreus to wait for when the flowers bloom again.
> **Castorice:** Now, Death is the power at my fingertips. It once led the souls of the deceased here, but I... I shall open the gate of the netherworld and return you to the realm of the living...
> **Castorice:** In exchange... Please, take the Coreflame of Death with you and complete the Flame-Chase Journey. Do not let our work thus far be in vain.
> *(Trailblazer)* You have my word.
> **Castorice:** Thank you.
> *(Trailblazer)* But you're not going back...
> *(Trailblazer)* You're... not going back?
> *(Trailblazer)* But at what cost?
> **Castorice:** Rest assured, "defying death" is Thanatos' divine authority. Fortunately, your soul hasn't dissipated. This is not a resurrection, but the extradition of a dying person back to the present world.
> *(Trailblazer)* So you're not going back...
> **Castorice:** Mm, just like the other demigods, I will stay here... and embrace my destiny.
> **Castorice:** That being said, it is not easy to guide a lost soul back to the mortal realm. The whispers of Death are sweet and alluring. If your determination wavers, you will succumb and be forever ensnared by the netherworld's grasp.
> **Castorice:** Please consider this a one-time exception. I will do everything in my power for the Flame-Chase Journey...
> **Castorice:** ...And for your life, the only life I can save.
> *(Trailblazer)* I'll do my best.
> **Castorice:** I am... glad to hear that.

>

**Announcer Fairy:** The long-awaited Memchelin Food Review is officially back —
**Announcer Fairy:** Lately, "(The Trailblazer's Hearth/Maze Grand Restaurant/Aedes Elysiae Community Canteen/Chrysos Heir Activity Center/Custom Name 2)" has been gaining tons of buzz in the Membrance Maze. With delicious food and warm, thoughtful service, they've won over the hearts of our fairy folk.
**Announcer Fairy:** The recently held Sweetie Festival was another big hit. Even I sneaked over for a bite. Mem! Words can't do it justice... It's just that good!
**Announcer Fairy:** Will they pass this latest round of judging? And what will the panel of expert reviewers have to say?
**Announcer Fairy:** Let's see what'll happen!
**Tribbie:** Little Gray, can **we** ask you a favor? Can **we** decide which dishes to submit for judging?
**Trianne:** Trianne wants to know whose dish is sweeter!
**Trinnon:** There happens to be three judges, so each of **us** only needs to prepare one dish.
> *(Trailblazer)* Alright, you've got my approval.
> *(Trailblazer)* Better bring your A-game, everyone.
> **Tribbie:** Thanks, Little Gray! Just place them on that empty table, right? **We**'ll go get everything ready right away!
**Judge Fairy:** Mem?
**Trianne:** Hey, that's not fair, picking the same dish as Trianne!
> *(Trailblazer)* Guess this is what they call telepathy.
> *(Trailblazer)* I kinda feel this is to be expected?
> **Tribbie:** **We** each had our own take on what the Sweetie Festival means.
> **Tribbie:** Even if it's all pancakes, the taste can still be totally different!
> **Castorice:** I see. To help the judges out, could each of you talk about your dish a little?
> **Trianne:** Trianne first! Sweet food's gotta be super sweet! So Trianne packed that pancake with a ton of honey.
> **Trianne:** Oh, and also, Trianne secretly asked De to learn a special technique!
> **Trianne:** Simmering it over and over till the honey turned extra rich and sweet, but not too heavy!
> **Trianne:** That sums up Trianne's idea! Sweet food should make people smile the moment they taste it!
> **Castorice:** So Trianne focused on improving the ingredients and techniques. What about the other two chefs?
> **Trinnon:** Trinnon read in a book that great restaurants explain to their guests how the food is made. It helps them enjoy it even more.
> **Trinnon:** The wheat used for these pancakes was carefully selected and cultivated by the restaurant's agricultural expert. Only the best made the cut after countless trials.
> **Trinnon:** And the head chef used a signature technique from Castrum Kremnos when kneading the dough and baking it, which makes the pancakes extra crisp and really brings out the full aroma of the honey.
> **Trinnon:** **We** wonder... does this flavor suit your palate?
> *(Trailblazer)* Look at you, really starting to sound like a pro waiter at a fancy establishment!
> *(Trailblazer)* Wow, didn't expect Trinnon to be this good at bragging...
> **Trianne:** Little Gray, Trinnon will let you in on a secret: Trinnon actually rehearsed that whole speech for days just for this!
> **Castorice:** You can tell a lot of love went into that dish. Now then, what's special about yours, Tribbie?
